War","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1d123dcc8b9214b39b7a","text":"Competition supplies the initiating comparison, resolve converts comparison into action, resistance supplies reciprocal force, and repeated war stabilizes opposition as an enduring social frame. Praise records the reputation won within that contest.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Lesion, Swelling, and Localized Pain","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1f618a01419a7508e438","text":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:2 `ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ` (`ع ل م`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `ٱلْمَغْضُوبِ` (`غ ض ب`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Daylight, Zenith, and Celestial Marking","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1f85dbb11a21596cfbc4","text":"Time is made intelligible by bounded spans and celestial positions, by pace within a duration, and by a culminating event of judgment.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Consecrated Offering","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_209e452eb73b1293802b","text":"Goods and obligations move between parties through ownership, valuation, replacement, compensation, gift, or offering.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Daylight, Zenith, and Celestial Marking","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_20c3e3db6cae3a8e9884","text":"A daylight span is located by the sun at zenith and by visible signs in the overhead sky.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Daylight, Zenith, and Celestial Marking","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_212666ac1241574c4635","text":"Daylight supplies the bounded interval, zenith gives it an internal midpoint, and sky signs provide a larger celestial coordinate system. Time is read through the positions of bodies that appear to stand overhead.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Engulfing and Penetrating Passage","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_219a700d6726ce447d5e","text":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); the worshipped deity `ء ل ه:B001/m02`; obedient religious submission `د ي ن:B001/m01`; humble worship and service `ع ب د:B003/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Wildlife Generation and Exposure","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2b2a1e25aa48b0cb41ad","text":"New animal life emerges from reproductive enclosure into an open field populated by birds, herd animals, and predators.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Covenant, Refuge, and Legal Protection","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2dd635b578103278e68f","text":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `غَيْرِ` and `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`غ ي ر`, `ن ع م`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Covenant, Refuge, and Legal Protection","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_395b9733f43d4b57c0a3","text":"Naming identifies the protected person, covenant establishes the relation, certification makes it publicly actionable, and indemnity defines the consequence of violating that protection.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"E:Protective Jealousy and Household Defense","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_39e150b132aa414d0f34","text":"Kinship supplies the protected circle, jealousy detects a threatened boundary, firmness supplies defensive capacity, and the frown externalizes readiness to resist.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Rout and Collective Dispersal","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_3a75288eb2ea00e4d866","text":"Confrontation supplies pressure, pursuit supplies direction, defeat breaks cohesion, and divergent routes express the spatial result. Postpartum pain preserves the bodily cost that follows the transition.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Worship and Obedient Submission","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_559780ef75bbdc5fbb95","text":"A dependent community turns toward a sovereign divine addressee through worship, service, obedience, and petition.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B001","root_000858/B001","root_001064/B001","root_001273/B008","root_001583/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Petition for Aid and Direction","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_57bb7384507f2bfc7480","text":"requested aid and cooperation `ع و ن:B001/m01`; The namesake motif defines identity comparatively by asking who else can bear the same designation.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Sky, Rain, Wind, and Enduring Growth","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_63e985af9bc5887e3bad","text":"Layered cloud gathers the water-bearing material, persistence and wind describe weather movement, irrigation delivers water to the ground, and green plant and old palm display growth across both season and duration.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Kinship, Fosterage, and Continuing Care","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_657673467963ca04b662","text":"Kinship is produced and maintained through gestation, nurture, marriage, covenant, and defense of household boundaries.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Engulfing and Penetrating Passage","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_69ed7b481ad163ae83be","text":"Swallowing, mounting, cutting, and burial share a directional transition across a boundary. Their common outcome is loss of exterior visibility, whether the receiving medium is a throat, a body, or the earth.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Wildlife Generation and Exposure","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6c647651be138699c006","text":"The womb and recently delivered ewe supply generation, the ostrich supplies a large bird of the open field, and falcon and hyena supply predatory animal classes. The stray marks the vulnerability that follows emergence beyond enclosure and custody.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B007","root_000532/B011","root_000745/B005","root_001119/B002","root_001583/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Covenant, Refuge, and Legal Protection","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_70e6310efbd287b90e81","text":"covenant and protected association `ر ب ب:B011/m01`;
